{"forms":[{"form":">","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","original_title":"Unsupported titles/`gt`","pos":"symbol","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"“i mean ubuntu is better than debian anyways, it's even the most mainstream version of desktop linux”\n“>ubuntu is better than debian”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to quote a person, especially to emphasize what was said and mock it."],"qualifier":"chiefly 4chan slang","tags":["Internet"]},{"examples":[{"text":"“and so i got a mac since they have less viruses[…]”\n“>macs are virus-free”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to quote a person, especially to emphasize what was said and mock it.","Used to emphasize and mock (what the speaker assumes is) someone's inner thoughts."],"qualifier":"chiefly 4chan slang","tags":["Internet"]},{"examples":[{"text":"“so i opened up internet explorer, went to google, and looked it up[…]”\n“>using internet explorer in 2026”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to quote a person, especially to emphasize what was said and mock it.","Used to emphasize and mock someone's actions."],"qualifier":"chiefly 4chan slang","tags":["Internet"]},{"examples":[{"text":">be me\n>decide to buy CS:GO to see why it's so popular\n>get instantly spawnkilled in my first game\n>uninstall the game and demand a refund","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to quote a person, especially to emphasize what was said and mock it.","Used for storytelling in short, fragmented statements."],"qualifier":"chiefly 4chan slang","tags":["Internet"]},{"examples":[{"text":"effort >","type":"example"},{"text":"random hugs >>>>>","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to signify enjoyment or appreciation of something (over everything else), often with many repetitions of the symbol."],"tags":["Internet"]}],"upstream_line":2496940,"upstream_offset":7584091387,"upstream_record_sha256":"56af87b48fd3a97838b2bbbb16d8614fcb1938e7715d6ed318636de373a057ab","word":"`gt`"}
